----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK18
Hangi Tablo iÁin : STOK18
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Sat˝nalma Sipari˛lerini Olu˛tur
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	LISTET.SafePack
	if LISTET.GetRecCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRecCount()
		if trim(LISTET.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) <> "" and trim(LISTET.TESLIM_ALINDIGI_TARH(i)) = "" Then
			MsgBox i & ". sat˝r teslim tarihi girilmemi˛. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir."
			Exit Sub
		End if
	Next

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46","")
	Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S46E.CH_KODU = LISTET.CHKODU(1)
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRecCount()
		if trim(LISTET.SEVK_TEDARIKCIDEN(i)) = "E" and trim(LISTET.TESLIM_ALINDI(i)) = "H" and _
				trim(LISTET.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)) <> "" Then
			S46T.AddRow
			satirNo = S46T.GetRecCount()
			S46T.KOD(satirNo) = LISTET.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S46T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = LISTET.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S46T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = LISTET.TESLIM_ALINDIGI_TARH(i)
			S46T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = LISTET.TESLIM_ALINDIGI_TARH(i)
			SiparisArtRef = Doc.getDBFieldValue("STOK46T", "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2", LISTET.SIPARTNO(i),"ARTNO")
			S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1(satirNo) = LISTET.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S46T.ARTIKEL_REF_REF_2(satirNo) = SiparisArtRef
			S46T.NOTE0(satirNo) = LISTET.SERINO(i)
			LISTET.TESLIM_ALINDI(i) = "E"
		End if
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 Then
		msgbox "Evrak kaydedilemedi"
	End if
	msgbox S46E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu sipari˛ yarat˝ld˝."

	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Doc.RunCommand "MT_SAVE"
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar ›Áin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Ayarlar˝
Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama iÁin : 
Bu Uygulama iÁin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu : 
Ba˛lang˝Áta Bu Makro «al˝˛acak : 
Alt Ekranlar ›Áin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK24
Hangi Tablo iÁin : STOK24
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Maliyetli Stok D¸zeltme Fi˛ine Maliyetleri Getirme (Fiili maliyetlerden)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	for i = 1 to STOK24T.GetRecCount()
		if trim(STOK24T.KOD(i)) <> "" then
			Maliyet = Doc.SonHesaplanMaliyet(STOK24T.KOD(i),STOK24E.AMBCODE,STOK24E.TARIH)
			STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
			STOK24T.OR_FIYAT(i) = Doc.Round(Maliyet,6)
			STOK24T.PUNIT_ORG(i) = Doc.DefaultPB
		end if
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar ›Áin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Ayarlar˝
Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama iÁin : 
Bu Uygulama iÁin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu : 
Ba˛lang˝Áta Bu Makro «al˝˛acak : 
Alt Ekranlar ›Áin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK24-1
Hangi Tablo iÁin : STOK24
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Maliyetli d¸zeltme fi˛ine maliyetleri getir (Fiyat Listesinden)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	STOK24T.SafePack()
	Set k = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	For i = 1 to STOK24T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Sat˝rlar fiyatland˝r˝l˝yor : " & i & " / " & STOK24T.GetRowCount()
		STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
		par1 = STOK24T.KOD(i)
		par2 = STOK24T.FOKOD(i)
		par3 = STOK24E.TARIH
		Fiyat = Doc.RunMacro3("MAKR7S.FIYAT", "GetFiyat", par1, par2, par3)
		STOK24T.OR_FIYAT(i) = Fiyat
		STOK24T.PUNIT_ORG = Doc.DefaultPB
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 7
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 210
	satirSonu = AltMargin - 30

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "BORDRO", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni,"")
	
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        

	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

	Dim Tarih1

	Dim IsyeriKodu

	ay1= R_BORDR1.AY(1)

	IsyeriKodu = R_BORDR1.ISYERIKODU(1)

		Msgbox "Dˆnem ve ›˛yeri Belirtmelisiniz"
		Exit Sub
	End If
	tarih1=yil  & "/" & ay1 & "/" & "01"
	tarih2=yil  & "/" & ay1 & "/" & "31"
	Dim Kriter
	dim Kriter1
	Dim Kriter3
	Kriter=""
	Kriter1=""
	Kriter3=""
	Kriter=Kriter&"CTARIH1>="&Tarih1
	Kriter=Kriter&" & "
	Kriter=Kriter&"CTARIH1<="&Tarih2
	Kriter=Kriter&" & "
	Kriter=Kriter&"ISYERIKODU="&Isyerikodu
	Dim Tbl_BRDR1TP
	Set Tbl_BRDR1TP=Doc.Select1("__BRDR1TP","BRDR1TP",Kriter,"",-1)
	Dim Tbl_BRDR1TP2
	Set Tbl_BRDR1TP2=Doc.Select1("__BRDR1TP2","BRDR1TP2",Kriter,"",-1)

	evrakno=Tbl_BRDR1TP.EVRAKNO
	Kriter1=Kriter1&"EVRAKNO="&evrakno

	Dim Tbl_BRDR1TPA
	Set Tbl_BRDR1TPA=Doc.Select1("__BRDR1TPA","BRDR1TPA",Kriter1,"",-1)

	Kriter3=Kriter3&"EVRAKNO="&evrakno
	Kriter3=Kriter3&" & "
	Kriter3=Kriter3&"KOD="&Isyerikodu

	Dim Tbl_BRDR1TI
	Set Tbl_BRDR1TI=Doc.Select1("__BRDR1TI","BRDR1TI",Kriter3,"",-1)
	Tehlike_Orani = 18 + Tbl_BRDR1TI.TEHLIKESINIFI_ORANI
	
	ay=Doc.getgecovalue("|MONTH2","AD",ay1,-1)
	PersonelSayisi=Tbl_BRDR1TP.GetRowCount

	NormalCalisma1 = 0 
	NormalCalisma2 = 0
	HaftaTatili1 = 0
	HaftaTatili2 = 0
	ResmiTatil1 = 0
	ResmiTatil2 = 0
	Yillilkizin1 = 0
	Yillilkizin2 = 0
	UcretliIzin1 = 0
	UcretliIzin2 = 0
	UcretsizIzin1 = 0
	UcretsizIzin2 = 0
	Devamsiz1 = 0 
	Devamsiz2 = 0
	Raporlu1 = 0
	Raporlu2 = 0
	Mesai50_1 = 0
	Mesai50_2 = 0
	Mesai100_1 = 0
	Mesai100_2 = 0
	MesaiToplami1 = 0	
	MesaiToplami2	= 0
	
	Ikramiye = 0
	Yakacak = 0
	Askerlik = 0
	Evlenme = 0
	Dogum = 0
	Olum = 0
	Egitim = 0
	Yemek = 0
	Izin = 0
	Yol = 0
	SDP = 0
	Kidem = 0
	Ihbar = 0

	for m = 1 to Tbl_BRDR1TP2.Getrowcount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "KazanÁlar Hesaplan˝yor : " & m & " / " & Tbl_BRDR1TP2.Getrowcount()
		if trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCG01" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCS01" Then
			NormalCalisma2 = NormalCalisma2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				NormalCalisma1 = NormalCalisma1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				NormalCalisma1 = NormalCalisma1 + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCG02" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCS02" Then
			HaftaTatili2 = HaftaTatili2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				HaftaTatili1 = HaftaTatili1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				HaftaTatili1 = HaftaTatili1 + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCG03" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCS03" Then
			ResmiTatil2 = ResmiTatil2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				ResmiTatil1  = ResmiTatil1  + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				ResmiTatil1  = ResmiTatil1  + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCG04" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCS04" Then
			Yillilkizin2 = Yillilkizin2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				Yillilkizin1  = Yillilkizin1  + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				Yillilkizin1  = Yillilkizin1  + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCG05" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KCS05" Then
			UcretliIzin2 = UcretliIzin2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				UcretliIzin1  = UcretliIzin1  + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				UcretliIzin1  = UcretliIzin1  + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KIG01" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KIS01" Then
			Raporlu2 = Raporlu2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				Raporlu1  = Raporlu1  + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				Raporlu1  = Raporlu1 + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KIG02" or trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KIS02" Then
			UcretsizIzin2 = UcretsizIzin2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
			if Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m) > 0 then
				UcretsizIzin1  = UcretsizIzin1  + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Else
				UcretsizIzin1  = UcretsizIzin1 + (Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)*7.5)
			End if

		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="KIG03" Then
			Devamsiz1 = Devamsiz1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_GUN(m)
			Devamsiz2 = Devamsiz2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="E01" Then
			Mesai50_1 =Mesai50_1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Mesai50_2 = Mesai50_2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="E02" Then
			Mesai100_1 =Mesai100_1 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.SURE_SAAT(m)
			Mesai100_2 = Mesai100_2 + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S01" Then
			Ikramiye = Ikramiye + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S02" Then
			Yakacak = Yakacak + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S03" Then
			Askerlik = Askerlik + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S04" Then
			Evlenme = Evlenme + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S05" Then
			Dogum = Dogum + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S06" Then
			Olum = Olum + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S07" Then
			Egitim = Egitim + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S08" Then
			Yemek = Yemek + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S09" Then
			Izin = Izin + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S10" Then
			Yol = Yol + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S11" Then
			SDP = SDP + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S12" Then
			Kidem = Kidem + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="S13" Then
			Ihbar = Ihbar + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="Y_DV" Then
			DVergisi = DVergisi + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)
		Elseif trim(Tbl_BRDR1TP2.KAZANCKESINTIKODU(m))="SENDIKA" Then
			Sendika = Sendika + Tbl_BRDR1TP2.HESAPLANAN_TUTAR(m)

		End if

	Next

	NormalKazanc = NormalCalisma2+HaftaTatili2+ResmiTatil2+	Yillilkizin2+UcretliIzin2
	VergiGun = NormalCalisma1+HaftaTatili1+ResmiTatil1+Yillilkizin1+UcretliIzin1+DogumIzni1+OlumIzni1
	MesaiToplami = Mesai50_2 + Mesai100_2
	MesaiToplami2 = Mesai50_1 + Mesai100_1
	SosyalYardimToplami = Ikramiye + Yakacak + Askerlik + Evlenme + Dogum + Olum + Egitim + Yemek + Izin + Yol + SDP + Kidem + Ihbar
	KazancToplami = NormalKazanc + MesaiToplami + SosyalYardimToplami

	res = pe1.setalign("Right")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 7,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(NormalCalisma2,20,2))

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(UcretliIzin2,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 70,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(NormalKazanc,20,2))

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai50_2,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai100_2,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 70,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(MesaiToplami,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Ikramiye,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Yakacak,20,2))

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 45,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Ihbar,20,2))

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 70,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(SosyalYardimToplami,20,2))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom + satiryuksekligi,solmargin + 70,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(KazancToplami,20,2))

	for i=1 to Tbl_BRDR1TP.Getrowcount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "SSK Bilgileri Hesaplan˝yor : " & i & " / " & Tbl_BRDR1TP2.Getrowcount()
			tarih1=Tbl_BRDR1TP.TARIH1(i)
			if trim(tarih1)<>"" then
				isegirensayisi=isegirensayisi+1
			end if

			tarih2=Tbl_BRDR1TP.TARIH2(i)
			if trim(tarih2)<>"" then
				istencikansayisi=istencikansayisi+1
			end if

			Cinsiyet=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0","CINSIYET",Tbl_BRDR1TP.KOD(i),"")
			Select case trim(Cinsiyet)
				case "K"
					k_sayisi = k_sayisi + 1
				case "E"
					e_sayisi=e_sayisi + 1
			End select

		sskmatrahi=sskmatrahi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_SSKMATRAHI(i)
		GVMatrahi=GVMatrahi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_GVMATRAHI(i)
		GelirVergisi=GelirVergisi + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_GV(i)
		SSKGun = SSKGun + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBORDRO_SSKPRIMGN_P(i)
		Maliyet = Maliyet + Tbl_BRDR1TP.PERSONELMALIYETI(i)	
		Borc = Borc + Tbl_BRDR1TP.KESILENBORC(i)
		Avans = Avans + Tbl_BRDR1TP.KESILENAVANS(i)

		IsciHastalik = IsciHastalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIH(i)
		IsciMaluliyet = IsciMaluliyet + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIM(i)
		IsciIssizlik = IsciIssizlik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMII(i)
		IsciSGDP = IsciSGDP + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMIS(i)
		IsciToplam = IsciHastalik + IsciMaluliyet
		IsciAlttoplam = IsciToplam + IsciSGDP 
		IsciGenelToplam = IsciToplam + IsciIssizlik
	
		IsvAnalik = IsvAnalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVA(i)
		IsvHastalik = IsvHastalik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVH(i)
		IsvMaluliyet = IsvMaluliyet + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVM(i)
		IsvIssizlik = IsvIssizlik + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVI(i)
		IsvSGDP = IsvSGDP + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVS(i)
		IsvMeslek = IsvMeslek + Tbl_BRDR1TP.BUBRDR_SSKPRIMVK(i) 
		IsvToplam = IsvAnalik + IsvHastalik + IsvMaluliyet + IsvMeslek
		IsvAltToplam = IsvToplam + IsvSGDP  	
		IssizlikToplam = IsciIssizlik + IsvIssizlik
		IsvGenelToplam = IsvToplam + IsvIssizlik
		SSK_Toplam = IsciGenelToplam + IsvGenelToplam
		SGDP_Toplam = IsciSGDP + IsvSGDP
		Toplam = SSK_Toplam + SGDP_Toplam
		
		KesintiToplami = Borc + Avans + Sendika
	Next

	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(UcretliIzin1,20,0) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Raporlu1,20,0) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(UcretsizIzin1,20,0) & " Saat")
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 95,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Devamsiz1,20,0) & " G¸n")

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + satiryuksekligi * 41,solmargin + 157,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai50_1,20,2) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 157,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(Mesai100_1,20,2) & " Saat")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getbottom,solmargin + 157,satiryuksekligi,20,doc.NumFormat0(MesaiToplami2,20,2) & " Saat")
 

End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar ›Áin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Ayarlar˝
Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama iÁin : 
Bu Uygulama iÁin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu : 
Ba˛lang˝Áta Bu Makro «al˝˛acak : 
Alt Ekranlar ›Áin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TEDARIK
Hangi Tablo iÁin : FOSN
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Sipari˛leri tedarikÁilere gˆre bˆl, mail gˆnder.
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=OTOSP1T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set SP = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set SP1T = Doc.GetTableObject("OTOSP1T")
	SP.CopyTable(SP1T)
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","OTOSP1T")
	for i = 1 to SP.GetRecCount()
		StokKodu = SP.KOD(i)
		GK9 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", StokKodu, "")
		SP.ARA_SEVK(i) = trim(GK9)
	Next
	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	SP.Sort("ARA_SEVK")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	EskiFirma = ""

	TsatirNo = 0
	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excele Yaz˝l˝yor : " & i & " / " & SP.GetRowCount()
		Firma = SP.ARA_SEVK(i)
		if trim(Firma) <> "" Then
			TsatirNo = TsatirNo + 1
			DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & trim(Firma) & ".xls"
			if EskiFirma <> Firma Then
				if TsatirNo <> 1 Then
					Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
					Wb.Save
					xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
					Wb.Close
					Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 9), xlsDosya)
				End if			
				Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
				Wb.SaveAs DosyaAdi
				Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
				satirNo = 1
				Sutun = 0
				for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
					AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
					if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
						Sutun = Sutun + 1

						Baslik = Grid(j,2)
						if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Baslik
					End if
				Next
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			End if
			Sutun = 0
			for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
				AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
				if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
					Sutun = Sutun + 1
					Baslik = Grid(j,2)
					if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For
					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = SP.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
				End if
			Next
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			EskiFirma = Firma
		End if
	Next

	if TsatirNo > 0 Then
		Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
		Wb.Save
		xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
		Wb.Close
		Excel.Quit
		Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 9), xlsDosya)
	End if
End Sub
Sub MailGonder(GrupKodu9, xlsDosya)
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(1,"h",-1,-1,"")	
	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.SetMailSubject("Ford Otosan Program - " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)

	Set r = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	mailAdresi = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK9", "AD", GrupKodu9, -1)
	if trim(mailAdresi) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Grup Kodu-9 : " & trim(GrupKodu9) & ", Grup Kodu aÁ˝klamas˝na mail adresi girilmemi˛.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.ClearMailReceivers()

	res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(trim(mailAdresi),1)
	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(xlsDosya,0)
	res = pe1.Maildoc()
	res = pe1.Close()
End Sub
Sub Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1), Sh1.Cells(1,256)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Range(Sh1.Cells(1,1), Sh1.Cells(1,2)).Select
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Hangi Tablo iÁin : STOK46
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Sat˝nalma Sipari˛lerini tedarikÁilere gˆre bˆl, mail gˆnder.
Kod : 
Public SP,stokkodu,firma
Sub Makro1()
	
	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","STOK46T")


	baslangicsiparis = InputBox("Ba˛lang˝Á Sat˝˛ Sipari˛inin Evrak Numaras˝n˝ Giriniz","Ba˛lang˝Á Sat˝˛ Sipari˛i ","")
	bitissiparis = InputBox("Biti˛ Sat˝˛ Sipari˛inin Evrak Numaras˝n˝ Giriniz","Biti˛ Sat˝˛ Sipari˛i ","")

	doc.cint(baslangicsiparis)	
	doc.cint(bitissiparis)

	if baslangicsiparis > bitissiparis then
		k=doc.d7msgbox("Yanl˝˛ evrak aral˝˝ verdiniz, L¸tfen biti˛ Evrak Numaras˝ Ba˛lang˝Á Evrak Numaras˝ndan B¸y¸k olmal˝","",MB_ICONWARNING)
		Exit sub
	end if

	QStr = "EVRAKNO>='"& Doc.RightJustify(trim(baslangicsiparis), 10) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & "&EVRAKNO<='"& Doc.RightJustify(trim(bitissiparis ), 10) & "'"
	Set SP = Doc.Select1("1ABC","STOK46T",QStr,"<EVRAKNO",-1)

		for i = 1 to SP.GetRecCount()
			Firma = SP.CH_KODU(i)
			Firmaadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Firma, "")
			SP.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i) = SP.EVRAKNO(i)
		Next


	if SP.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	SP.Sort("CH_KODU")
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	EskiFirma = ""

	TsatirNo = 0

	For i = 1 to SP.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excele Yaz˝l˝yor : " & i & " / " & SP.GetRowCount()
		stokkodu = SP.KOD(i)
		Firma = SP.CH_KODU(i)
		Firmaadi = trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Firma, ""))

		if trim(Firma) <> "" Then
			TsatirNo = TsatirNo + 1
			DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & LEFT(trim(Firmaadi),10) & ".xls"
			if EskiFirma <> Firma Then
				if TsatirNo <> 1 Then
					Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
					Wb.Save
					xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
					Wb.Close
					Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 10), xlsDosya)
				End if			
				Set Wb = Excel.Workbooks.Add
				Wb.SaveAs DosyaAdi
				Set Sh1 = Wb.Sheets(1)
				satirNo = 1
				Sutun = 0
				for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
					AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
					if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or AlanAdi ="SF_MIKTAR" or AlanAdi ="SF_SF_UNIT" or AlanAdi ="SF_VRI_VR_R3" or AlanAdi ="REV_RTESTARIH" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
						Sutun = Sutun + 1
						Baslik = Grid(j,2)
						if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Baslik
							if AlanAdi = "SF_VRI_VR_R3" Then
								Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = "Sipari˛ No"
							Else
								Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Value = Baslik
							End if
					End if
				Next
				satirNo = satirNo + 1
			End if
			Sutun = 0
			for j = 0 to uBound(Grid,1)
				AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)
					if AlanAdi = "KOD" or AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" or AlanAdi ="SF_MIKTAR" or AlanAdi ="SF_SF_UNIT" or AlanAdi ="SF_VRI_VR_R3" or AlanAdi ="REV_RTESTARIH" or InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPCOL") > 0 Then
					Sutun = Sutun + 1
					Baslik = Grid(j,2)
					if inStr(1, Baslik, "...") > 0 Then Exit For

					if AlanAdi = "KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", SP.KOD(i), "")
					Else
						Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = SP.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)
					End if
				End if
			Next
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			EskiFirma = Firma
		End if
	Next

	if TsatirNo > 0 Then
		Call Formatla(Excel, Wb, Sh1)
		Wb.Save
		xlsDosya = Wb.FullName
		Wb.Close
		Excel.Quit
		Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 10), xlsDosya)
	End if
End Sub
'Sub MailGonder(GrupKodu9, xlsDosya)
Sub MailGonder(firma, xlsDosya)
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(1,"h",-1,-1,"")
	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.SetMailSubject(Firmaadi & " Program - " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))
	res = pe1.SetMailWithDialog(1)

	Set r = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
'	mailAdresi = Doc.GetGecoValue("STKGK9", "AD", GrupKodu9, -1)
	mailAdresi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "EMAIL", firma, "")

	if trim(mailAdresi) = "" Then
'		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Grup Kodu-9 : " & stokkodu & ", Stok Kodlu ¸r¸n¸n Grup Kodu aÁ˝klamas˝na mail adresi girilmemi˛.", "", MB_OK
		Doc.d7MsgBox "E-Posta : " & firma & ", Cari Hesap Kodlu firman˝n " & "'" & "e-Mail"& "'" &" alan˝na mail adresi girilmemi˛.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.ClearMailReceivers()

	res = pe1.AddMailReceiver(trim(mailAdresi),1)
	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(xlsDosya,0)
	res = pe1.Maildoc()
	res = pe1.Close()
End Sub

			Firma = "TT"
			
			  if trim(Firma) <> "" Then
			TsatirNo = TsatirNo + 1
			   DosyaAdi = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "ABDAT\" & Replace(Doc.BuGun, "/", "") & "-" & LEFT(trim(Firma),10) & ".xls"

			End if

'			End if
				End if

		End if

			AlanAdi = Grid(j,0)

					Sh1.Cells(satirNo, Sutun).Formula = S29T.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, i)

			End if
		Next

		satirNo = satirNo + 1
		EskiFirma = Firma
		  End if
	End if
	
Next

	Call MailGonder(Left(EskiFirma, 10), xlsDosya)

		Exit Sub

	res = pe1.ClearMailAttachments()
	res = pe1.ClearMailReceivers()

	res = pe1.AddMailAttachment(xlsDosya,0)

Sub Makro1()
	STOK19T.SafePack
	if trim(STOK19E.SHIPTOCODE) = "" or trim(STOK19E.CHKODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve sevk adres kodunu girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	For i = 1 to STOK19T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Malzeme listesi okunuyor : " & i & " / " & STOK19T.GetRecCount()
		ST40.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40.KOD(satirNo) = STOK19T.KOD(i)
		ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK19T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = STOK19E.TARIH
		ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = STOK19E.TARIH
		ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = STOK19E.SHIPTOCODE
	Next
	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")	
	ST40.Sort("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("ML", STOK19E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO",0)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
	EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak y¸klenemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= STOK19E.CHKODU
	ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	ST40E.SEVKADRESI_1 = STOK19E.SEVKADRESI_1

	ST40T.Empty

	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		if i = 100 then exit sub
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sat˝˛ sipari˛ine aktar˝l˝yor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	Msgbox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipari˛ yarat˝ld˝"
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
	Dim T_STOK19E    
	Set T_STOK19E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK19E")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","STOK19E",T_STOK19E.EVRAKNO)   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	if trim(TNT00E.TESLIM) = "" or trim(TNT00E.CH_KODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve teslim yerini girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	For i = 1 to TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "TNT Y¸kleme Formu Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		if TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i) > 0 Then
			ST40.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = TNT00T.KOD(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = TNT00E.TESLIM
		end if
	Next
	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")	
	ST40.Sort("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("TNT00E", TNT00E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO",0)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
	EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak y¸klenemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
	ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
	ST40E.CHSIPNO = TNT00E.EVRAKNO
	ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""

	ST40T.Empty

	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.AddRow
		satirNo = ST40T.GetRecCount()
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sat˝˛ sipari˛ine aktar˝l˝yor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		Exit Sub
	end if
	nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo)
	Msgbox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipari˛ yarat˝ld˝"
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo)
 Dim T_TNT00E    
 Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")   
 Dim nret    
 nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)   
 Dim T_U1    
 Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
 K = T_U1.SetFieldValue("SIPARISNO",-1,SiparisNo)   
 T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar ›Áin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Ayarlar˝
Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama iÁin : 
Bu Uygulama iÁin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu : 
Ba˛lang˝Áta Bu Makro «al˝˛acak : 
Alt Ekranlar ›Áin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : TNT3
Hangi Tablo iÁin : TNT00
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : TNT y¸kleme formunu sipari˛ine aktarma
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(TNT00E.TESLIM) = "" or trim(TNT00E.CH_KODU) = "" then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox("Cari hesap kodu ve teslim yerini girmelisiniz." & vbcrlf & "›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
		exit sub
	end if
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK40T","TMPCOPY"
	Set ST40 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")

	Set f = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Hata = ""
	For i = 1 to TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "TNT Y¸kleme Formu Okunuyor : " & i & " / " & TNT00T.GetRecCount()
		if TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i) > 0 Then
			ST40.AddRow
			satirNo = ST40.GetRecCount()
			ST40.KOD(satirNo) = TNT00T.KOD(i)
			ST40.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = TNT00T.SF_MIKTAR_ONAYLNMS(i)
			ST40.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = TNT00E.YUKLEMETARIHI
			ST40.CHSIPNO(satirNo) = TNT00E.TESLIM
			Gk4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_4", TNT00T.KOD(i), "")
			if InStr(1, TNT00E.TESLIM, "D1D4F") > 0 and trim(Gk4) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox TNT00T.KOD(i) & " iÁin Grup Kodu-4 girilmemi˛", "", MB_OK
				Hata = "x"
			End if
			ST40.NOTES(satirNo) = Gk4
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Hata) <> "" Then Exit Sub

	ST40.GroupBy("REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO+KOD")	
	ST40.Sort("NOTES+REV_RTESTARIH+CHSIPNO")	

	if ST40.GetRecCount = 0 then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK40","")
	Set ST40E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40E")
	Set ST40T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK40T")

	EvrakSiraNo = -1
	For i = 1 to ST40.GetRecCount()
		Gk4 = ST40.NOTES(i)
		if Gk4 <> EskiGk4 Then
			if trim(ST40E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
				if nret = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipari˛ yarat˝ld˝", "", MB_OK
					Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo, EvrakSiraNo)
				End if
			End if
			EvrakSiraNo = EvrakSiraNo + 1
			Call YeniSipVeyaYukle(Doc1, EvrakSiraNo)
			EvrakNo = ST40E.EVRAKNO
			if nret <> 0 then
				k = doc.d7MsgBox(EvrakNo & " no.lu evrak y¸klenemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
				Exit Sub
			end if
			ST40E.MUSTERIKODU= TNT00E.CH_KODU
			ST40E.SHIPTOCODE = trim(ST40.CHSIPNO(1))
			ST40E.CHSIPNO = TNT00E.EVRAKNO
			ST40E.FIYSEKLI = ""

			ST40T.Empty
		End if

		satirNo = ST40T.AddRow
		ST40T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Sat˝˛ sipari˛ine aktar˝l˝yor : " & EvrakNo & " - " & i & " / " & ST40.GetRecCount()

		ST40T.KOD(satirNo) = ST40.KOD(i)
		ST40T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = ST40.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		ST40T.RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.REV_RTESTARIH(satirNo) = ST40.REV_RTESTARIH(i)
		ST40T.NOTES(satirNo) = ST40.NOTES(i)
		EskiGk4 = Gk4
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret <> 0 then
		k = doc.d7MsgBox(ST40E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu evrak kaydedilemedi. ›˛lem kesilmi˛tir","dinamo",MB_OK)
	Else
		nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		Call KTA_Yaz(EvrakNo, EvrakSiraNo)
		Doc.d7MsgBox EvrakNo & " no.lu sipari˛ yarat˝ld˝", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub KTA_Yaz(SiparisNo, EvrakSiraNo)
	Dim T_TNT00E    
	Set T_TNT00E = Doc.GetTableObject("TNT00E")   
	Dim nret    
	nret = Doc.UFLD00_CREATEEDITTABLE("T_U0001","TNT00E",T_TNT00E.EVRAKNO)   
	Dim T_U1    
	Set T_U1 = Doc.GetTableObject("T_U0001")   
	if EvrakSiraNo = 0 Then
		AlanAdi = "SIPARISNO"
	Else
		AlanAdi = "SIPARISNO_" & EvrakSiraNo
	End if
	K = T_U1.SetFieldValue(AlanAdi,-1,SiparisNo)   
	T_U1.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub
Sub YeniSipVeyaYukle(Doc1, EvrakSiraNo)
	SiparisNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("TNT00E", TNT00E.EVRAKNO, "SIPARISNO", EvrakSiraNo)
	if trim(SiparisNo) = "" Then
		nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	else
		if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","EVRAKNO", Doc.RightJustify(trim(SiparisNo),10),"")) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(SiparisNo)
		Else
			nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
		End if
	end if
End Sub

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : YPUANTAJ
Hangi Tablo iÁin : BORDRO1
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : DOSTEL PUANTAJ-YEN›
Kod : 
Glo_path="C:\ISA\HAZ›RAN.XLS"
Glo_satirsayisi = 300
Sub Makro1()

	colNormalCalisma = 4
	colHaftaTatili = 7
	colGenelTatil = 8
	colYillikIzin = 13
	colUcretliIzin = 14
	colDogumIzni = 18
	colOlumIzni = 19
	colEvlenmeIzni = 20
	colDoktorIzni = 23
	colSendikaIzni = 24
	colUcretsizIzin = 15
	colIstirahat = 17
	colMesai50 = 9
	colMesai100 = 12
	colNormalCalismasiGece = 5
	colMesai50Gece = 10
	colMesai100Gece = 11


	dim strFpath,intSatirSayisi
	Fpath = Glo_path
	strFpath = inputbox("Excel dosyas˝n˝n yolunu giriniz","Puantaj dosyas˝n˝n yolu",Glo_path)
	if trim(strFpath)="" Then 
		msgbox("Bo˛ dosya ad˝ olamaz.Makroyu tekrar Áal˝˛t˝r˝n.")
		exit Sub
	End if
	intSatirSayisi = inputbox("Excel dosyas˝ndan okunacak sat˝r say˝s˝","Excel'deki sat˝r say˝s˝",Glo_satirsayisi)
	if not isNumeric(intSatirSayisi) Then 
		msgbox("Bo˛ ya da yanl˝˛ sat˝r bildirimi.Makroyu tekrar Áal˝˛t˝r˝n.")
		exit Sub
	End if
	dim objExcel
	Set objExcel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

	dim objWorkbooks
	Set objWorkbooks = objExcel.Workbooks
	objWorkbooks.Open(strFpath)
	
	dim objSheet1
	Set objSheet1 = objWorkbooks(1).Worksheets(1)
	dim c,strPersNo
	dim dblNormalCalisma,dblHaftatatili,dblGenelTatil,dblYillikizin,dblucretliizin,dbldogum
	dim dblOlum,dblEvlenme,dblDoktor,dblSendika
	dim dblIstirahat,dblucretsiz,dbl100mesai,dbl50mesai

	if err.number<>0 Then 
		objWorkbooks(1).Close
		objExcel.Quit
		Set objExcel=Nothing
		Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
		Set objSheet1=Nothing
		Exit Sub
	End if	

	For c=1 to BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Puantaj Bilgileri Aktar˝l˝yor : " & c & " / " & BRDR1PBGIRIS.GetRecCount
		'strPersNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		SicilNo = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_KOD(c)
		strPersNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("PERSM0","EHLIYETNO",Sicilno,"")
		BRDR1PBGIRIS.SetCurrentRow(c)
		if TRUE Then
			Sutun=1
			For ik=2 to Glo_satirsayisi
				if Trim(objSheet1.Cells(ik,sutun))=trim(strPersNo) Then
					satir = ik
					Exit For
				End if
			Next

			strPersNoExcel = objSheet1.Cells(satir,sutun).Value
			i = satir
	
			if trim(strPersNoExcel) <> "" Then
				if Trim(strPersNoExcel)=Trim(strPersNo) Then

					'NORMAL «ALIﬁMA
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalisma).Value) Then
						dblNormalCalisma=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalisma).Value)
					Else
						dblNormalCalisma=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'HAFTA TAT›L›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colHaftaTatili).Value) Then
						dblHaftatatili=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colHaftaTatili).Value)
					Else
						dblhaftatatili=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'GENEL TAT›L
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colGenelTatil).Value) Then
						dblGenelTatil=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colGenelTatil).Value)
					Else
						dblGenelTatil=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'YILLIK IZIN
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colYillikIzin).Value) Then
						dblYillikizin=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colYillikIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblYillikizin=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'‹CRETL› ›Z›N 
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretliIzin).Value) Then
						dblucretliizin=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretliIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblucretliizin=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'DO–UM ›ZN›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDogumIzni).Value) Then
						dblDogum=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDogumIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblDogum=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'÷L‹M ›ZN›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colOlumIzni).Value) Then
						dblOlum=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colOlumIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblOlum=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'EVLENME ›ZN›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colEvlenmeIzni).Value) Then
						dblEvlenme=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colEvlenmeIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblEvlenme=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'DOKTOR ›ZN›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDoktorIzni).Value) Then
						dblDoktor=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colDoktorIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblDoktor=cdbl(0)
					End if
				'SEND›KA ›ZN›
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colSendikaIzni).Value) Then
						dblSendika=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colSendikaIzni).Value)
					Else
						dblSendika=cdbl(0)
					End if

 					'‹CRETS›Z ›Z›N
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretsizIzin).Value) Then
						dblucretsiz=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colUcretsizIzin).Value)
					Else
						dblucretsiz=cdbl(0)
					End if
 					'›ST›RAHAT
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colIstirahat).Value) Then
						dblIstirahat=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colIstirahat).Value)
					Else
						dblIstirahat=cdbl(0)
					End if

					'%50 MESAI (SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50).Value) Then
						dbl50mesai=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50).Value)
					Else
						dbl50mesai=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'%100 MESAI(SAAT)
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100).Value) Then 
						dbl100mesai=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100).Value)
					Else
						dbl100mesai=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Normal «al˝˛ma
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalismasiGece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceCalisma=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colNormalCalismasiGece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceCalisma=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Mesai %50
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50Gece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceMesai50=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai50Gece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceMesai50=cdbl(0)
					End if
					'Gece Mesai %100
					if isnumeric(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100Gece).Value) Then 
						dblGeceMesai100=cdbl(objSheet1.Cells(i,colMesai100Gece).Value)
					Else
						dblGeceMesai100=cdbl(0)
					End if

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS01(c) = dblNormalCalisma
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS02(c) = dblHaftatatili
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS03(c) = dblGenelTatil
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS04(c) = dblYillikizin
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS05(c) = dblucretliizin-dblDoktor
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS06(c) = dbldogum
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS07(c) = dblOlum
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS08(c) = dblEvlenme
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS09(c) = dblDoktor
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KCS10(c) = dblSendika

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIS01(c) = dblIstirahat
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AS_KIS02(c) = dblucretsiz

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E01(c) = dbl50mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E02(c) = dbl100mesai
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E03(c) = dblGeceCalisma
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E04(c) = dblGeceMesai50
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_E_AS_E05(c) = dblGeceMesai100


					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = Doc.div(dblNormalCalisma,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c) = Doc.div(dblHaftatatili,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c) = Doc.div(dblGenelTatil,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) = Doc.div(dblYillikizin,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c) = Doc.div(dblucretliizin,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c) = Doc.div(dbldogum,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) = Doc.div(dblOlum,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c) = Doc.div(dblEvlenme,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c) = Doc.div(dblDoktor,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c) = Doc.div(dblSendika,7.5)

					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS01(c) = Doc.div(dblIstirahat,7.5)
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS02(c) = Doc.div(dblUcretsiz,7.5)
		
					'G¸nleri tam deer yap
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c))
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c))		
					BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c) = 	doc.round(BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c))		

		
					SSKToplami = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS02(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS03(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS04(c) _ 
					+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS05(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS06(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS07(c) _
					+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS08(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS09(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS10(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS01(c)+BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KIS02(c)
						
					GirilmesiGirenSSK = BRDR1PBGIRIS.C_GEREKEN_SSKPRIMGUNU(c) 

					if SSKToplami > GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark = SSKToplami - GirilmesiGirenSSK
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)-Fark
					End if
					if SSKToplami < GirilmesiGirenSSK then
						Fark1 = GirilmesiGirenSSK - SSKToplami
						BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c) = BRDR1PBGIRIS.B_K_AP_KCS01(c)+Fark1
					End if
				

				End if
			End if
'			if err.number<>0 Then 
			if False Then 
				MSGBOX "Hata " & Err.Number & " " & Error()
				objWorkbooks(1).Close
				objExcel.Quit
				Exit Sub
				Set objExcel=Nothing
				Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
				Set objSheet1=Nothing
			End if	
		End if
		if False Then 
			objWorkbooks(1).Close
			objExcel.Quit
			Set objExcel=Nothing
			Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
			Set objSheet1=Nothing
			Exit Sub
		End if	
	Next
	
	objWorkbooks(1).Close
	objExcel.Quit

	Set objExcel=Nothing
	Set objWorkbooks=Nothing
	Set objSheet1=Nothing
	msgbox "Dosya aktarma i˛lemi bitti."
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar ›Áin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Ayarlar˝
Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama iÁin : 
Bu Uygulama iÁin Ba˛lang˝Á Ekran Formu : 
Ba˛lang˝Áta Bu Makro «al˝˛acak : 
Alt Ekranlar ›Áin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran AÁ˝lmadan ÷nce «al˝˛t˝r˝lacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : DOST10 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : sql
Hangi Tablo iÁin : REP17S
›lave Hangi Uygulamalar iÁin : 
AÁ˝klama : Rapor SQL komutunu yarat
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	REP17RT.SafePack
	REP17JT.SafePack
	REP17KT.SafePack
	SQLStr = ""

	SelectStr = GetSelectStr()
	SQLStr = SQLStr & SelectStr

	JoinStr = GetJoinStr()
	if trim(JoinStr) <> "" Then
		SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & JoinStr
	End if

	WhereStr = GetWhereStr()
	if trim(WhereStr) <> "" Then
		SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & WhereStr
	End if

	REP17SE_B.SQLTEXT = SQLStr
End Sub
Function GetSelectStr()
	SQLStr = "SELECT "
	k = 1
	For i = 1 to REP17RT.GetRowCount()
		if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
			if BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
				Alan = GetOrijinalAlanAdi(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & " " & trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i))
			Else
				Alan = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i))
			End if
			if Len(SQLStr) > k * 90 Then
				SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & Chr(9)
				k = k + 1
			End if
			SQLStr = SQLStr & trim(Alan) & ", "
		End if
	Next
	For i = 1 to REP17KT.GetRowCount()
		if not RaporIcerigindeVarmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
			if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
				if BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) Then
					Alan = GetOrijinalAlanAdi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & " " & trim(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i))
				Else
					Alan = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i))
				End if
				if Len(SQLStr) > k * 90 Then
					SQLStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & Chr(9)
					k = k + 1
				End if
				SQLStr = SQLStr & trim(Alan) & ", "
			End if
		End if
	Next

	if Right(trim(SQLStr), 1) = "," Then
		SQLStr = Left(SQLStr, Len(SQLStr) - 2)
	End if
	
	GetSelectStr = SQLStr & vbcrlf & "FROM " & trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE)
End Function
Function GetWhereStr()
	if REP17KT.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		GetWhereStr = ""
		Exit Function
	End if
	WhereStr = "WHERE 1=1 "
	For i = 1 to REP17KT.GetRowCount()
		if IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) and not EklenenAlanmi(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i)) _
				and Doc.Is_EY1(REP17KT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			WStr = CreateWhereStr(REP17KT.FIELDNAME1(i), REP17KT.KRITERTIPI(i))
			if WStr <> "" Then
				WhereStr = WhereStr & vbcrlf & WStr
			End if
		End if
	Next
	GetWhereStr = WhereStr
End Function
Function CreateWhereStr(AlanAdi, KriterTipi)
	if not BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(AlanAdi) Then
		AlanAdi1 = trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE) & "." & trim(AlanAdi)
	Else
		AlanAdi1 = trim(AlanAdi)
	End if
	if trim(KriterTipi) = "><" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">=###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = ">=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">=###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = ">" Then
		CreateWhereStr = " AND " & AlanAdi1 & ">###KRITERGIRIS.B_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<=###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<###KRITERGIRIS.E_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.EQ_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "=###KRITERGIRIS.EQ_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "<>" or trim(KriterTipi) = "!=" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.NE_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>###KRITERGIRIS.NE_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & _
					"###ENDIF###"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "BOﬁ" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND " & AlanAdi1 & "=''"
	Elseif trim(KriterTipi) = "!BOﬁ" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>''"
	Elseif Left(KriterTipi,1) = "S" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND (" & GetCLStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1) & ")"
	Elseif Left(KriterTipi,2) = "NS" Then
		CreateWhereStr = "AND (" & GetNSStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1) & ")"
	End if
End Function
Function GetJoinStr()
	GetJoinStr = ""
	if IcmaldenOnceJoinYok Then Exit Function

	JoinStr = ""
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			if JoinStr <> "" Then
				JoinStr = JoinStr & vbcrlf & "INNER JOIN "
			Else
				JoinStr = "INNER JOIN "
			End if
			JoinStr = JoinStr & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & " ON "
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "."
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.JOINEDFIELD(i))
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "="
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17SE.AU_TABLE)
			JoinStr = JoinStr & "."
			JoinStr = JoinStr & Trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i))
		End if
	Next
	GetJoinStr = JoinStr
End Function
Function IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi(AlanAdi)
	IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi = True
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if len(Baglanti) > Len(AlanAdi) Then
			Exit Function
		Else
			if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti and Doc.Is_HN0(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
				IcmaldenOnceBaglantimi = False				
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function BaglantidanGelenAlanmi(AlanAdi)
	BaglantidanGelenAlanmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if len(Baglanti) > Len(AlanAdi) Then
			Exit Function
		Else
			if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti and Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
				BaglantidanGelenAlanmi = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetOrijinalAlanAdi(AlanAdi)
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		Baglanti = trim(REP17JT.FIELDNAME1(i)) & "_" & trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
		if Left(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)) = Baglanti Then
			TabloAdi = trim(REP17JT.JOINEDTABLE(i))
			BaglantidanGelenAlan = Mid(AlanAdi, Len(Baglanti)+2)
			GetOrijinalAlanAdi = TabloAdi & "." & trim(BaglantidanGelenAlan)
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function EklenenAlanmi(AlanAdi)
	EklenenAlanmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17UT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(REP17UT.FIELDNAME1(i)) = trim(AlanAdi) Then
			EklenenAlanmi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function IcmaldenOnceJoinYok()
	IcmaldenOnceJoinYok = True
	For i = 1 to REP17JT.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.Is_EY1(REP17JT.BEFOREGROUPBY(i)) Then
			IcmaldenOnceJoinYok = False				
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function RaporIcerigindeVarmi(AlanAdi)
	RaporIcerigindeVarmi = False
	For i = 1 to REP17RT.GetRowCount()
		if trim(REP17RT.FIELDNAME1(i)) = trim(AlanAdi) Then
			RaporIcerigindeVarmi = True
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function GetCLStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1)
	AlanSayisi = Doc.CDbl(Mid(KriterTipi, 2))
	OrStr = "1=1 " & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	For i = 1 to AlanSayisi
		OrStr = OrStr & "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.CL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"OR " & AlanAdi1 & "=###KRITERGIRIS.CL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"###ENDIF###" & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	Next
	GetCLStr = OrStr
End Function
Function GetNSStr(KriterTipi, Alanadi, AlanAdi1)
	AlanSayisi = Doc.CDbl(Mid(KriterTipi, 3))
	NSStr = "1=1 " & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	For i = 1 to AlanSayisi
		NSStr = NSStr & "###IF NOTEMPTY KRITERGIRIS.NCL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(Alanadi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"AND " & AlanAdi1 & "<>###KRITERGIRIS.NCL" & Right("00" & i, 2) &"_" & trim(AlanAdi) & "###" & vbcrlf & chr(9) & _
			"###ENDIF###" & vbcrlf & chr(9)
	Next
	GetNSStr = NSStr
End Function
